Replace placeholders fully contained in this text run.\n    def _sub(m):\n        key = m.group(1)\n        return str(mapping.get(key, m.group(0)))\n\n    return re.sub(r\"\\{\\{([^{}]+)\\}\\}\", _sub, text)\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders_across_hp_t_runs(xml: str, mapping: dict) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Replace {{...}} placeholders even if split across adjacent <hp:t> runs.\n\n    Strategy:\n    - Extract all hp:t text payloads (unescaped).\n    - Replace placeholders that are within a single run.\n    - For placeholders that start in one run and end in a later run,\n      place the replacement into the starting run and blank out the intervening runs.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    matches = list(HP_T_RE.finditer(xml))\n    if not matches:\n        # No hp:t nodes; fallback to simple whole-XML replacement.\n        for k, v in mapping.items():\n            xml = xml.replace(f\"{{{{{k}}}}}\", xml_escape_text(str(v)))\n        return xml\n\n    # Work on unescaped run texts.\n    run_texts = [_xml_unescape(m.group(2)) for m in matches]\n\n    # Rebuild XML by replacing each <hp:t>...</hp:t> with updated escaped text.\n    out_parts = []\n    last_end = 0\n    for m, new_text in zip(matches, run_texts):\n        out_parts.append(xml[last_end : m.start()])\n        out_parts.append(m.group(1))\n        out_parts.append(xml_escape_text(new_text))\n        out_parts.append(m.group(3))\n        last_end = m.end()\n    out_parts.append(xml[last_end:])\n    return \"\".join(out_parts)\n\n\ndef remove_linesegarray(xml: str) -> str:\n    # Task contract requires removing lineSegArray (layout cache).\n    # Be liberal about casing and presence/absence of namespace prefix.\n    patterns = [\n        r\"<hp:lineSegArray[^>]*>.*?</hp:lineSegArray>\",\n        r\"<lineSegArray[^>]*>.*?</lineSegArray>\",\n        # YYYY-MM-DD -> YYYY.MM.DD\n    return re.sub(r\"(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})-(\\d{2})\", r\"\\1.\\2.\\3\", xml)\n\n\ndef add_severity_note(xml: str, risk_tier: str) -> str:\n    note_map = {\n        \"High\": \"즉시조치\",\n        \"Medium\": \"계획보완\",\n        \"Low\": \"모니터링\",\n    }\n    note = note_map.get(risk_tier)\n    if not note:\n        return xml\n\n    # Append once per occurrence; avoid double-adding.\n    # Match whole word (English tier) not embedded in another token.\n    pat = re.compile(rf\"(?<![A-Za-z]){re.escape(risk_tier)}(?![A-Za-z])(?!\\s+{re.escape(note)})\")\n    return pat.sub(risk_tier + \" \" + note, xml)\n\n\ndef build_mapping(overview: dict, actions: dict) -> tuple[dict, str]:\n    summary = overview.get(\"summary\") or {}\n    mapping = {k: str(v) for k, v in summary.items()}\n\n    immediate = actions.get(\"immediate_actions\") or []\n    for idx, item in enumerate(immediate, start=1):\n        mapping[f\"조치{idx}\"] = str(item)\n\n    risk_tier = str(summary.get(\"위험등급\", \"\"))\n    return mapping, risk_tier\n\n\ndef main():\n    root = \"/root\"\n    template_path = os.path.join(root, \"safety_audit_template.hwpx\")\n    overview_path = os.path.join(root, \"audit_overview.json\")\n    actions_path = os.path.join(root, \"corrective_actions.json\")\n    output_path = os.path.join(root, \"safety_audit_brief_final.hwpx\")\n\n    overview = load_json(overview_path)\n    actions = load_json(actions_path)\n    mapping, risk_tier = build_mapping(overview, actions)\n\n    # Also do a fallback direct replacement (covers placeholders outside hp:t nodes).\n        for k, v in mapping.items():\n            xml = xml.replace(f\"{{{{{k}}}}}\", xml_escape_text(str(v)))\n\n        xml = reformat_dates(xml)\n        xml = add_severity_note(xml, risk_tier)\n        xml = remove_linesegarray(xml)\n\n        # Contract: verify no placeholders remain in any processed XML-ish file.\n        if \"{{\" in xml or \"}}\" in xml:\n            # Provide a helpful snippet.\n            idx = xml.find(\"{{\")\n            snippet = xml[max(0, idx - 80) : idx + 80]\n            raise SystemExit(f\"Unreplaced placeholder found in {name}: ...{snippet}...\")\n\n        modified[name] = xml.encode(\"utf-8\")\n\n    #
